# 🌌 Alloy Pairs — The End parents (1/2)

> 📖 Part of the **[Alloy Recipe Index](Alloy-Recipe-Index.md)**. The rules, the formulas and the 12 essence effects live on that page.

Every pair whose **first parent** (the id that comes first alphabetically) is a **The End** material. Each section shows the material's stats and trait, the list of **all** its partners and the detailed rows for the ones that sort after it.

> 🔎 **How to search:** press **Ctrl+F** and type the id (`mvtink_tin`). If it is not on this page, use the page table in the [Alloy Recipe Index](Alloy-Recipe-Index.md).

> 🧪 **Effect shorthand:** under each row's essence links, the small line says what that essence does on **weapon / tool / armor**. A **composite trait** (for example `Preservation-Adamant Edge`) is only a name: the real mechanics are the inherited essences. The **16 legendary** recipes, however, do carry their own curated effect.

---

### Adamantium · `mvtink_adamantium`

- **Stats:** +3200 · 11x · +6
- **Trait:** Indestructible — 75% chance to negate durability consumption.
- **Essences:** [Void](Alloy-Recipe-Index.md#void), [Tempered](Alloy-Recipe-Index.md#tempered), [Swift](Alloy-Recipe-Index.md#swift)
- **Blends with (109):** Adamite, Aether Pearl, Aetherium, Amber, Amethyst, Amethyst Geode Crystal, Ancient Debris Slag, Aquamarine, Ardite, Astralite, Bauxite, Beryllium, Bismuth, Blackstone Pyrite, Blazesteel Shard, Borax, Pure Calcite, Celestine, Chorus Crystal, Chromite, Chrono Crystal, Cinderite, Cinnabar, Coal, Cobalt, Copper, Cosmium, Crimson Gold, Crimson Quartz, Cryolite, Cursed Brimstone, Diamond, Dragon Scale Shard, Eclipse Gem, Emerald, End Crystal Shard, Enderite, Fire Opal, Flint, Fluorite, Galena, Ghast Tear Shard, Glowstone Gem, Gold, Graphite, Gravitite, Gypsum, Helliron, Ignis Ferrum, Infernal Obsidian, Netherite-Infused Quartz, Iron, Jade, Kaolinite, Lapis Lazuli, Lapis Matrix, Magma Brimstone, Magmacite, Magnetite, Malachite, Nebulite, Nether Bismuth, Nether Tungsten, Netherite Scrap Shard, Nickel, Null-Shard, Obsidian, Obsidianite, Opal, Ender Pearl Core, Phantomite, Platinum, Prismarine, Pyrite, Pyrophore, Nether Quartz, Redstone, Resonite, Ruby, Sanguinite, Sapphire, Shadowgem, Shulkerite, Silver, Singularite, Soul Glass Crystal, Spatial Platinum, Starlight Silver, Steel, Stibnite, Sulfur, Talc, Tesseract Crystal, Tin, Titanium, Topaz, Tourmaline, Tungsten, Void Pyrite, Void Titanium, Voidstone, Compacted Volcanic Ash, Warped Emerald, Warped Quartz, Pure Weeping Shard, Witherite, Zero-Point Shard, Zinc, Zircon
